**Direct memory access**

- Burst mode transfers all data in a contiguous block before releasing bus control, making CPU inactive for extended periods.
- Scatter-gather DMA transfers data to/from multiple memory areas in a single transaction, offloading multiple I/O tasks from CPU.
- Cache must be flushed before DMA reads or invalidated before DMA writes to prevent stale data access.

<details><summary>2 more lessons</summary>

</details>
